T016 coverage rationale (RJ): records group continuing choices rather than commits. Formal descriptive completeness (D-031), executable-root ownership (D-037), module placement (D-030), authoring mechanics (D-034/D-036), quality authority (D-020/D-053), measured adoption (D-025) and proposed marginal-value comparison (D-065) remain distinct even where the same component appears. Scope-compatible repetitions, clerical edits and proven alternate presentations need not create decisions. Dates are 2026 throughout; “onward” denotes retained documentary framing, not a universal no-change assertion.

T016 evidence completion: the accepted ADR at already-accounted 44d734f0 was read to distinguish its material semantic choices, and the Gen2 review at already-accounted 923cdfac was read for its exact structural-versus-executable qualification. E-0525 CD, E-0526 RD and E-0527 RD supplement existing anchors and are linked to those same COVERAGE rows. No new candidate or disposition change; no implementation was opened.

### Cross-thread causality audit — T019

The T-23 “trigger” sentence was corrected to documentary sequence: the retained anchors did not require a cross-thread causal claim about the wrong-corpus report. No temporal sequence is used as an unqualified cause.

## I. Transfer Audits

T021 seeded X-01–X-24 before BLIND; the completed post-challenge records below replace those seeds under the same IDs. Linked E-IDs identify exact documentary commits/paths and §D retains lineage context. The edited-out coordinator-onboarding claim (E-0400 CD/E-0401 CD; E-0512 CM) is not treated as a fictional source owner; L24 presentation equivalences are not independent retirements.

### T032 transfer judgments

T032 completed 2026-09-07. Each record follows the FR-037 chain and has a final bounded reviewer judgment. Selected preservation, deliberate historical compression, an explanatory-pointer mismatch and DOC-only verification limits are distinguished. A claimed source/test destination is not inspected as evidence. X-24 is a conditional proposal, not an executed transfer. Validation: X-01–X-24 unique/contiguous, nine chain fields each, all E/D/T/RC references resolve; E-0001–E-0533 contiguous, E-0533 linked to its already-covered commit. Corpus/disposition totals unchanged; no late candidate discovered. BLIND seal matches; REPORT absent; canonical staged and unstaged diffs empty. T033 is next.

6. **documentary paths / historical path families searched:** Committed AGENTS.md, README.md, and docs/codebase-review/{04-api-and-http-contracts.md,05-search-and-retrieval-architecture.md,07-current-gaps-and-roadmap.md,ARCHITECTURE_DECISIONS_OBSERVED.md,REVIEW_NOTES.md}. These are exact committed snapshots already inside the frozen universe; neither current remote state nor other dates are claimed searched by this record.

### Post-BLIND challenge receipt — T029

T029 completed 2026-09-07. The user supplied H1–H16 after T027 sealing and the T028 external stop. These are adversarial inputs, not historical evidence or predetermined findings. Receipt now unlocks T030. The externally resolved operational HEAD mismatch changed neither the frozen corpus nor the sealed synthesis; no factual correction to BLIND is warranted.

The exact challenge classification vocabulary is: STRONGLY SUPPORTED; PARTIALLY SUPPORTED; NOT SUPPORTED; DISPROVED; INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE. Classification is separate from severity and does not automatically create a final finding. Classifications will be assigned only after active disconfirmation at T030.

| H-ID / supplied label | Normalized supplied hypothesis |
| --- | --- |
| H1 — Source-of-truth drift | Repeated movement of current-state, architecture, status, and operational authority among roadmap, handoff, README, AGENTS, live plans, specs and operations created material source-of-truth drift or periods in which a reader could reasonably encounter competing documentary owners. |
| H2 — Temporary artifacts outliving their temporary role | Some temporary plans, review documents, migration artifacts, checklists or milestone owners survived beyond the problem they were created to solve and became quasi-permanent documentary machinery. |
| H3 — Roadmap / handoff / status leakage | Roadmap, handoff and current-status material was duplicated across multiple owners often enough that stale status or repeated milestone narration leaked between surfaces and required later cleanup. |
| H4 — Migration vocabulary surviving the migration | Migration/cutover/temporary/compatibility terminology and machinery persisted after the relevant transition had materially completed, making the documentary architecture harder to understand as a current-state system. |
| H5 — Proof/evaluation machinery expanded beyond demonstrated value need | Evidence collection, proof gates, report schemas, protected acceptance, operator workflows and governance expanded substantially, potentially faster than documentary evidence of marginal product/search value. |
| H6 — Safety constraint became a substitute for the product objective | No-harm/baseline-preservation may at times have shifted from a constraint on a useful search improvement into the primary success objective, while incremental relevance/value remained weakly demonstrated. |
| H7 — Distinct Qdrant decisions were sometimes collapsed into one story | Historical discussion may have conflated several separate Qdrant-related questions: whether semantic retrieval should exist; what it should retrieve; whether/how it should supplement baseline results; lifecycle/resource readiness; activation/exposure; evaluation/value; production permission. Some apparent reversals may disappear when these are treated as separate Decision Threads. The documentary conflation claim and the analytical benefit of separation must both be tested using the independently derived T-xx model. |
| H8 — Reusable-platform ambition exceeded production-domain evidence | The project may have invested in generalized reusable search/platform architecture beyond what was justified by concrete production-domain adoption, with neutral fixtures or second-domain proofs carrying more weight than actual second production consumers. |
| H9 — Generated/derived versus handwritten authority was unclear | Authority between generated/derived declarations, handwritten specs, schemas/runtime declarations, plans and narrative docs changed enough that a reader could be unsure which representation was normative. |
| H10 — Process/governance shaped architecture materially | Coordinator workflow, delegated-agent rules, proof policy and review-process constraints materially influenced architectural/documentary shape, rather than merely documenting an independently chosen architecture. |
| H11 — Evidence/artifact ownership was unstable | Ownership and durability of evaluation evidence, generated artifacts, temporary workspaces and review bundles changed repeatedly enough to create reproducibility/source-identity risk. |
| H12 — Later ownership corrections reveal substantive earlier mismatch | Repeated corrections that moved declarations, schemas, DTOs, contracts, status owners or documentary authority to different locations reflect genuine earlier ownership mistakes, not merely cosmetic refactoring. |
| H13 — Rule preservation outperformed rationale preservation | Consolidations and retirements often preserved current rules or successor pointers while losing the rationale, alternatives, rejected options, historical status or explanation that made those rules understandable. |
| H14 — Requirement-strength drift was sometimes implicit | Some requirements moved between proposal/optional/default/required/forbidden or temporary/current/permanent states without a sufficiently explicit documentary decision explaining the strength transition. |
| H15 — Important chronology became discoverable mainly through Git archaeology | After final-state compression and retirement of historical layers, some important architectural rationale/status chronology became understandable only by reconstructing Git history rather than by reading the surviving documentary architecture. |
| H16 — Simplification pressure reflects real complexity, but the final proposal is not proof | The terminal simplification proposal and preceding consolidation history may indicate that architectural/documentary/proof complexity had accumulated enough to merit reduction. However, the simplification document is explicitly UNAPPROVED and Q2-gated. Therefore it is evidence of a proposed diagnosis/alternative, NOT documentary proof that the prior architecture was objectively overengineered. |

Search obligations retained from the supplied pack: challenge owner drift with explicit transfers/deduplication; distinguish durable history from accidental persistence; distinguish stale migration framing from live contracts and open work; assess concrete safety/attribution reasons and actual value evidence rather than quantity; keep safety, quality, marginal value and permission distinct; do not treat neutral fixtures as production adoption; require explicit cross-thread causal linkage; distinguish evidence moves from loss and identity improvements; test each correction/strength change against its contemporary rationale; audit rule/rationale/alternatives/status/orphaning separately; reserve current-reader necessity to T035; treat the final proposal as conditional. Every hypothesis requires strongest counter-evidence and an active disconfirming search.

No BLIND content is changed.

T030 validation: 16 unique ordered H-records, each with the normalized hypothesis, one allowed classification and all required support/search/counter-evidence/uncertainty/impact fields; E/D/T/RC/X references resolve. Results: STRONGLY SUPPORTED 3; PARTIALLY SUPPORTED 7; NOT SUPPORTED 3; DISPROVED 0; INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE 3. Distribution was not a target. E-0531/E-0532 remain the only post-BLIND additions, both CD on covered commits; evidence total 532, candidate/disposition delta zero, no late candidate unresolved. No factual correction to BLIND is required. SHA-256 remains `e1bc7e45c50aee34fc04a603e27a5e94fd50201e80bde082722394693b025406`; REPORT remains absent. T032 is next.

#### H1 — Source-of-truth drift

- Normalized hypothesis: Repeated movement of current-state, architecture, status, and operational authority among roadmap, handoff, README, AGENTS, live plans, specs and operations created material source-of-truth drift or periods in which a reader could reasonably encounter competing documentary owners.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — The June review index retains provisional production questions alongside narrower guides (E-0132 CD); a later reconciliation explicitly corrects stale backend/current-run statements across handoff, roadmap and review notes (E-0188 CD). HI — these are bounded competing-state episodes, stronger evidence than owner movement alone.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — Handovers name distinct owners, and the Gen2 sole-live-plan rule is applied by deleting README's duplicate status on first use (E-0174 CD; E-0414 CD; E-0447 CD; E-0448 CD). CD — the final README/spec/operations/post-cutover split is explicit (E-0509 CD; E-0522 CD). HI — this weakens a claim of enduring or universal source-truth failure.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: look for role separation, same-change transfers and removal of duplicates that explain apparent competition. Searched the evidence ledger for current owner/source, sole, superseded, historical and retained; compared frozen README with the 56dc5086 README and c8481ca1 dated-status clarification already reopened. Inspected E-0132/E-0188 against E-0405/E-0447/E-0448. Result: mixed historical snapshots and successful explicit transfers coexist; pointers and dated records cannot be counted as competing authority.
- Decision/audit links: D-004/D-018; T-04/T-14/T-44; RC-04/06; X-06/X-11/X-14/X-21/X-22.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — no reader-impact frequency or universal synchronization claim is established. Exact transfer completeness remains for T032 and current-reader sufficiency for T035; changes did not by themselves prove causation.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — narrows BF-03 to demonstrated historical statement conflicts and strengthens PF-05/PF-06. It does not turn the owner-movement history into a general defect.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: a bounded historical status/authority-reconciliation candidate, potentially combined with H3; also a positive consolidation candidate. No F-ID assigned yet.
- Material absence claim: No absence claim is needed for the positive conflicts/corrections. An eventual assertion that a transfer lacked a decision would require a separate bounded A-record.

#### H2 — Temporary artifacts outliving their temporary role

- Normalized hypothesis: Some temporary plans, review documents, migration artifacts, checklists or milestone owners survived beyond the problem they were created to solve and became quasi-permanent documentary machinery.
- Final challenge classification: **NOT SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — The module-split plan becomes a cumulative current-state/history ledger after its initial frozen planning role (E-0379 CD; E-0405 CD); review and migration owners sometimes remain as reference material until later retirement (E-0128 RD; E-0447 CD; E-0507 CD). HI — role extension is visible, but does not establish accidental quasi-permanence.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — dated snapshots are deliberately qualified; compatibility retirement is gated by usage; temporary plans, manual checklist and closed delivery owners are explicitly retired or superseded (E-0383 CD; E-0405 CD; E-0299 CD; E-0437 CD; E-0497 CD). CD — remaining post-cutover work has an active owner, and the proposal distinguishes historical inventory from live proof (E-0522 CD; E-0521 CD).
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: test whether allegedly overlong artifacts still had an explicit historical, compatibility, live-proof or unfinished-work role. Reused the owner/temporary/supersession ledger search, the c8481ca1 dated record, and frozen technical-spec historical/current cutover matches. Compared lifecycle closure E-0234 with open work, and Gen2 retirement E-0497 with E-0522. Result: concrete intentional roles and retirements undermine accidental-permanence inference; longevity itself supplies no defect.
- Decision/audit links: D-021/D-032/D-040/D-059/D-066; T-19/T-27/T-31/T-44; RC-11/14/19; X-08/X-10/X-15/X-19/X-21.
- Residual uncertainty: RJ — this is not a proof that every temporary artifact retired at an optimal time. The material 'beyond its role' and 'quasi-permanent' parts are not established by the selected duration/role examples.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — prevents BF-04/BF-07 becoming a blanket temporary-machinery finding; retains PF-08 and the candidate value of deliberate retirement.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: No negative permanence finding from this hypothesis. Bounded intentional-retirement positives may converge with other evidence at T034.
- Material absence claim: No. This classification does not rely on claiming that no stale artifact ever existed.

#### H3 — Roadmap / handoff / status leakage

- Normalized hypothesis: Roadmap, handoff and current-status material was duplicated across multiple owners often enough that stale status or repeated milestone narration leaked between surfaces and required later cleanup.
- Final challenge classification: **STRONGLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — E-0188 CD explicitly fixes stale backend/current-run wording across several current-state surfaces; E-0213 CD synchronizes six review owners after wrapper retirement and replaces stale HTTP characterizations; E-0448 CD removes README's duplicated brick state. HI — multiple concrete episodes support leakage/cleanup, not just repeated links.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — the live-plan rule demands same-commit updates and pointer-only summaries (E-0447 CD), and first-use cleanup follows it (E-0448 CD). CD — final README removes duplicated module material and directs current status to the remaining-work plan (E-0509 CD; E-0522 CD). HI — documented successful cleanup limits any claim that leakage remained the endpoint condition.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: distinguish benign navigation from conflicting or repeated live facts, and look for completed deduplication. Reused frozen README, 56dc5086 README and the current-owner ledger search; compared E-0188/E-0213 actual correction descriptions with E-0447/E-0448 pointer-only status. Result: some repetition is benign, but the explicit stale-state repairs and duplicate-state deletion survive that distinction.
- Decision/audit links: D-003/D-018; T-03/T-04/T-15; RC-06; X-06/X-11/X-14/X-21/X-22.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — the claim is supported as recurring bounded documentary repair, not as a measured error rate, false test result, implementation defect or uninterrupted current failure.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — strengthens the concrete historical part of BF-03 while preserving PF-05/PF-06. Count-location conflict remains a different rule-placement issue, not evidence of false verification.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: consolidate with H1 into a bounded stale-state/ownership-maintenance finding, plus successful-correction evidence. Do not create one finding for each cleanup.
- Material absence claim: No; the claim rests on inspected corrections and duplicate removal.

#### H4 — Migration vocabulary surviving the migration

- Normalized hypothesis: Migration/cutover/temporary/compatibility terminology and machinery persisted after the relevant transition had materially completed, making the documentary architecture harder to understand as a current-state system.
- Final challenge classification: **NOT SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — migration-derived names and typed completed-cutover evidence remain in the final technical specification (E-0509 CD); older typed-GET plans were described as stale by a later review (E-0128 RD). HI — persistence of terminology is established, but harmful current migration framing does not follow.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — dated flags are explicitly historical (E-0405 CD); actual compatibility consumers constrain deletion (E-0383 CD); Gen1 documentation retires with cutover (E-0493 CD; E-0494 CD). CD — current cutover invariants and retained live proof are distinguished from historical inventory (E-0509 CD; E-0521 CD), while Q2 is explicitly unfinished (E-0522 CD).
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: search surviving technical-spec migration/cutover/compatibility/temporary occurrences for historical labels, live invariants or still-open work. The already-completed frozen-spec scan and frozen README owner read show those distinctions explicitly; c8481ca1 shows deliberate snapshot preservation. Compared the retirement and successor anchors rather than treating names as proof. Result: the selected surviving vocabulary has defensible roles; the hypothesis's present-understanding harm is unestablished.
- Decision/audit links: D-003/D-032/D-040/D-059/D-066; T-03/T-27/T-31/T-44; RC-03/11/14/19; X-05/X-15/X-20/X-21/X-24.
- Residual uncertainty: RJ — T035 must still assess actual current-reader necessity and local awkward wording. This classification does not certify absence of every stale phrase or infer implementation deletion from documentary assertions.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — constrains BF-04/BF-08; supports preserving PF-08/PF-10 rather than treating live cutover proofs as obsolete by name.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: No generalized migration-residue finding. A later local documentary defect requires its own material evidence and §M entry.
- Material absence claim: No; no corpus-wide absence of migration residue is asserted.

#### H5 — Proof/evaluation machinery expanded beyond demonstrated value need

- Normalized hypothesis: Evidence collection, proof gates, report schemas, protected acceptance, operator workflows and governance expanded substantially, potentially faster than documentary evidence of marginal product/search value.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — Offline artifact identities/schemas and readiness/approval models expand into later protected-acceptance and source-attribution obligations (E-0195 CD; E-0200 CD; E-0256 CD; E-0288 CD; E-0503 CD; E-0522 CD). CD — M17 explicitly distinguishes completed foundations from the original hybrid goal (E-0303 CD). HI — documentary proof-work expansion and an acknowledged delivery gap are supported; a comparative growth rate or excess is not measured.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — lifecycle-before-shadow has an explicit comparison-validity rationale (E-0201 CD); failed/noisy supplement alternatives and observed improvement remain recorded (E-0314 CD; E-0326 CD); resource cancellation prevents false closure, and holdout failures trigger visible disclosure/replenishment (E-0475 CD; E-0504 CD). CD — old reporting layers are retired and the proposal protects live proof (E-0329 CD; E-0521 CD).
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: look for concrete failure boundaries, product observations and simplifications that could justify the machinery. Reused measured/quality/approval/neutrality ledger search, frozen technical-spec evaluation/cutover ranges and proposal live-proof/firewall corrections. Compared E-0303 with real-improvement/no-harm requirements and E-0504 disclosures. Result: explicit safety/attribution/overfitting reasons and some measured benefit defeat a volume-equals-excess inference.
- Decision/audit links: D-019/D-020/D-024/D-025/D-058/D-060/D-064; T-15/T-17/T-18/T-22/T-23/T-42; RC-08/09/17/18; X-11/X-19/X-23/X-24.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — time/cost, marginal relevance across the product, and whether any proof process was self-perpetuating remain unverified. RD proposal burden claims are not implementation/economic findings (E-0520 RD).
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — retains BF-07's distinct documentary complexity question and BF-05's bounded value uncertainty; strengthens PF-02/PF-03/PF-10. It blocks an objective-overengineering headline.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: scoped foundation-versus-delivery interpretation and positive proof-scope discipline may seed findings. No finding of excessive machinery follows from this classification alone.
- Material absence claim: Not for the supported expansion/correction. A claim that value evidence was missing throughout the corpus would need an A-record and is not made.

#### H6 — Safety constraint became a substitute for the product objective

- Normalized hypothesis: No-harm/baseline-preservation may at times have shifted from a constraint on a useful search improvement into the primary success objective, while incremental relevance/value remained weakly demonstrated.
- Final challenge classification: **NOT SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — foundation/gate milestones close before the original hybrid goal, and a worked supplement policy preserves one useful case while losing another (E-0303 CD; E-0314 CD). RD — the terminal proposal questions whether safety establishes marginal value (E-0520 RD). HI — these support incomplete value demonstration, not a declared replacement of the product objective by safety.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — evidence-only adoption explicitly rejects hybrid by faith and noisy artifacts (E-0311 CD); the neutral supplement gate requires an improvement as well as zero regressions (E-0334 CD); a bounded accepted checkpoint records one improved query (E-0326 CD); cutover still requires relevance evidence (E-0530 CD). CD — proposal asks for a same-source product-value decision rather than treating safety as sufficient (E-0521 CD).
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: look for a positive-improvement requirement and distinctions between safety, evaluation, permission and product value. Reused frozen spec's cutover/evaluation read, the measured-improvement ledger search and proposal G5 alternatives. Compared original-goal correction E-0303 with E-0311/E-0334 and RC-18. Result: improvement remains an explicit obligation; foundation closure is not represented as full product delivery after correction.
- Decision/audit links: D-020/D-024/D-025/D-049/D-065; T-10/T-18/T-22/T-23; RC-09/18; X-12/X-13/X-20.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — the actual magnitude and generality of incremental value remain unresolved. An improvement-observed append and a controlled baseline/full relevance delta are different, but that distinction does not prove value abandonment.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — preserves BF-05's uncertainty while rejecting its expansion into safety-as-substitute; retains PF-01/PF-02 and gives positive-improvement criteria explicit weight.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: No negative objective-substitution finding. A bounded finding distinguishing safety, benefit and permission may survive T034 without alleging substitution.
- Material absence claim: No. Neither absence of value nor absence of evaluation is asserted.

#### H7 — Distinct Qdrant decisions were sometimes collapsed into one story

- Normalized hypothesis: Historical discussion may have conflated several separate Qdrant-related questions: whether semantic retrieval should exist; what it should retrieve; whether/how it should supplement baseline results; lifecycle/resource readiness; activation/exposure; evaluation/value; production permission. Some apparent reversals may disappear when these are treated as separate Decision Threads. The documentary conflation claim and the analytical benefit of separation must both be tested using the independently derived T-xx model.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — early ES-only production phrasing and later no-binding characterization can blur intended policy with exposure (E-0082 CD; E-0125 CD); managed-local default permission is later qualified separately from production approval (E-0338 CD; E-0339 CD). HI — separating existing threads resolves some apparent reversals. This supports the pack's analytical-separation clause more strongly than its claim that documents conflated the decisions.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — vector intent coverage is explicitly not hybrid readiness (E-0047 CD); candidate IDs, full-result duties and score channels are separated (E-0050 CD; E-0102 CD); quality evidence, approval request and approved opt-in are distinct (E-0254 CD; E-0256 CD; E-0262 CD); accepted Gen2 contracts retain separate baseline/candidate roles (E-0525 CD).
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: find contemporaneous explicit separations before attributing a unified Qdrant story to the corpus. Reused quality-versus-activation search and frozen evaluation/cutover ranges; compared E-0047/E-0050/E-0256 with actual default-scope changes, using the pre-existing T-08/T-10/T-11/T-12/T-13/T-18/T-23/T-24 boundaries. Result: many distinctions were maintained explicitly; the supplied taxonomy is unnecessary and has not replaced the thread model.
- Decision/audit links: D-008/D-010/D-011/D-012/D-013/D-020/D-025/D-027/D-050; T-08/T-10/T-11/T-12/T-13/T-14/T-18/T-23/T-24; RC-07/18; X-04/X-13/X-20.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — generalized historical conflation is not established. Terminological scope ambiguity remains in specific exposure/default passages; separate-thread coherence does not prove actual runtime states.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — strengthens BF-01/BF-05's separation of questions and PF-02; weakens any reading that compresses all Qdrant evolution into one reversal.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Potentially a positive explicit-boundary finding and a bounded terminology clarification, not a new Qdrant meta-thread or standalone universal conflation finding.
- Material absence claim: No; the argument uses affirmative documentary distinctions.

#### H8 — Reusable-platform ambition exceeded production-domain evidence

- Normalized hypothesis: The project may have invested in generalized reusable search/platform architecture beyond what was justified by concrete production-domain adoption, with neutral fixtures or second-domain proofs carrying more weight than actual second production consumers.
- Final challenge classification: **INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE**
- Strongest support: CD — the documented DAG, generic boundaries, neutral tracer and per-brick authoring obligations extend beyond immediate domain-only descriptions (E-0355 CD; E-0411 CD; E-0452 CD; E-0455 CD). RD — the proposal questions packaging/proof cost under a single-consumer premise (E-0520 RD). HI — these establish ambition and a later cost judgment, not that investment exceeded justified need.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — original principles explicitly permit a pure neutral component without a second production adopter, require a real domain requirement and independent fixture, and forbid fixture-only universal/vocabulary claims (E-0531 CD). CD — early one-domain limitations and explicit business projection persist (E-0033 CD; E-0434 CD), and Article evidence is test-level (E-0118 CD). RD — the proposal itself affirms fixtures and the firewall's negative property (E-0520 RD).
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: test whether the corpus actually makes production adoption a prerequisite or overstates fixture evidence. Reused original 8666a15a principles reopen (E-0531 CD), the neutral/second-domain ledger search and proposal corrections. Compared reference-consumer wording with deferred D1 (E-0524 CD; E-0522 CD). Result: an adopter prerequisite is explicitly rejected under bounded conditions; reference wording is not evidence of a second adopter.
- Decision/audit links: D-002/D-030/D-038/D-062/D-069; T-02/T-26/T-29; RC-17; X-16/X-18/X-24.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — production adoption count, economic cost, alternative implementation cost and the necessity of physical project granularity have not been established by DOC-only evidence. No corpus-wide only-one-domain claim is made.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — materially strengthens PF-07 and the reuse qualifications behind PF-03; constrains BF-06 and BF-07. The challenge exposes the invalid inference from limited adoption evidence to unjustified pure neutrality.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: No overinvestment finding on this basis. Yes to a supported positive finding about evidence-qualified reuse and explicit limits, if retained under T034.
- Material absence claim: Only if a future finding depends on no second production adopter. That premise is unnecessary for the present classification and is not asserted.

#### H9 — Generated/derived versus handwritten authority was unclear

- Normalized hypothesis: Authority between generated/derived declarations, handwritten specs, schemas/runtime declarations, plans and narrative docs changed enough that a reader could be unsure which representation was normative.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — The early single-source claim for BeautySearchSpec is expressly narrowed when schema and runtime owners are separated (E-0030 CD; E-0351 CD; E-0352 CD). CD — a full aggregate declaration records intentional semantic omissions, and a later canonical reading-path claim is narrowed until missing links are restored (E-0381 CD; E-0486 CD). HI — these support bounded ambiguity about the extent/completeness of a declared authority, rather than independent competing generated authorities.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — The relocation itself names the new owners (E-0351 CD; E-0352 CD). CD — the authoring principles explicitly prohibit derived registries, traces, ledgers and documentation tables from becoming independent authorities (E-0531 CD). RD — later review distinguishes structural assembly from executable serving completeness (E-0527 RD); that distinction must not be read back as evidence of an uninspected implementation defect.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: look for explicit derivation and source-truth rules that make apparent owner competition a legitimate separation of layers. Reused frozen technical-spec derived/current wording and original 8666a15a principles inspection; compared E-0030/E-0352 with the aggregate's stated omissions and E-0486's self-narrowing. Result: normative direction is explicit; the surviving issue is breadth/completeness terminology at particular checkpoints, not a demonstrated free choice between generated and handwritten truth.
- Decision/audit links: D-006/D-031/D-037/D-069; T-06/T-28/T-29/T-44; RC-12/16; X-16/X-18/X-19/X-24.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — actual generator/schema agreement and reader confusion are not established. Structural completeness, executable coverage and documentary authority remain separate questions.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — narrows BF-02 to scoped completeness claims and supports PF-06/PF-07. It rejects a general generated-versus-handwritten authority failure inferred from relocation alone.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: a scoped completeness/authority-language candidate may converge with H12. Positive derivation discipline is also eligible; no separate blanket authority finding.
- Material absence claim: No for the affirmative narrowing and source rules. An assertion that an authority had no explanation would require a bounded A-record.

#### H10 — Process/governance shaped architecture materially

- Normalized hypothesis: Coordinator workflow, delegated-agent rules, proof policy and review-process constraints materially influenced architectural/documentary shape, rather than merely documenting an independently chosen architecture.
- Final challenge classification: **STRONGLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — Governance explicitly requires boundary declarations before edits (E-0353 CD), per-brick authoring-result records and derived views (E-0455 CD), a pre-5D audit gate (E-0476 CD), and its replacement by named cleanup/materialization/navigation decisions (E-0479 CD). CD — the audit-derived acceptance requirement is then extended to remaining bricks (E-0486 CD). HI — these explicit documentary links support material influence on prescribed architectural work and documentary shape.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — technical rationales also explain lifecycle-before-shadow, module placement and the side-by-side strategy independently of a general governance story (E-0201 CD; E-0410 CD; E-0447 CD). CM — E-0510 CM supplies an incident motive for a governance rule, and E-0511 CM records a workflow deviation; neither proves actual downstream architecture or a universal causal account.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: require an explicit process-to-design link, then seek technical rationales that could explain the same changes. Reused governance/ownership and lifecycle/module-placement searches, T019 causality corrections and the CM/CD comparison; inspected E-0455/E-0476/E-0479/E-0486 against E-0201/E-0410/E-0447. Result: specific documentary links survive; temporal co-evolution alone is discarded.
- Decision/audit links: D-016/D-017/D-030/D-038/D-058; T-16/T-26/T-02/T-15; RC-14/15/16; X-06/X-07/X-15/X-19.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — this classification applies to explicit prescriptions and documented decision links, not observed implementation causality, net benefit/harm, or all architectural decisions. CM incident claims remain commit metadata.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — strengthens the bounded governance dimension of BF-07 while preserving PF-07/PF-09. Process influence is not itself a defect.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: a bounded documentary-governance influence finding or explanatory result, potentially positive/mixed. No finding that governance caused overall architectural excess.
- Material absence claim: No; the causal scope is supported by affirmative links.

#### H11 — Evidence/artifact ownership was unstable

- Normalized hypothesis: Ownership and durability of evaluation evidence, generated artifacts, temporary workspaces and review bundles changed repeatedly enough to create reproducibility/source-identity risk.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — The post-cutover record identifies unavailable protected prerequisites, disposable local copies, canonical-resource tracking and source-revision requirements (E-0503 CD; E-0508 CD; E-0522 CD). HI — reproducibility and source identity were material risks explicitly addressed; ownership movements alone are not the evidence of risk.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — persistent evidence moves deliberately from stable user-owned storage to project-local workspaces and later repository-local audit runs (E-0516 CD; E-0517 CD; E-0506 CD). CD — canonical tracked inputs require a newly reproduced audit after change, and immutable application revision/restart/independent verify rules strengthen attribution (E-0508 CD; E-0522 CD; E-0523 CD). HI — these support an intentional durability progression rather than established orphaning.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: look for retained input identities, restoration instructions and source-change rules that make path changes safe or corrective. Reused workspace/revision/immutable-source ledger search and frozen post-cutover policy inspection; compared the two June workspace rules with E-0506/E-0508 and final candidate/promotion/verify separation. Result: explicit safeguards and durability improvements weaken the hypothesis's movement-caused-risk reading.
- Decision/audit links: D-019/D-060/D-061/D-064; T-17/T-42/T-43; RC-19; X-07/X-11/X-23.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — no actual evidence loss, irreproducible run or orphaned bundle is demonstrated here. The necessity of source identity is documented; frequency and realized impact of attribution failures are not established.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — retains BF-07's artifact/proof distinctions and strengthens PF-03/PF-09. It prevents path churn from being treated as proof of lost evidence.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: a positive durability/source-attribution correction candidate and bounded explanation of prerequisites. No loss finding on this record.
- Material absence claim: No. A future claim that artifacts were unrecoverable would require affirmative loss evidence or a bounded A-record; neither is asserted.

#### H12 — Later ownership corrections reveal substantive earlier mismatch

- Normalized hypothesis: Repeated corrections that moved declarations, schemas, DTOs, contracts, status owners or documentary authority to different locations reflect genuine earlier ownership mistakes, not merely cosmetic refactoring.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — Exact semantic-text duplication is centralized with intentional exceptions retained (E-0400 CD); DTO ownership is reversed to align with documented consumers (E-0410 CD); canonical navigation and trusted materialization boundaries receive explicit cleanup decisions (E-0479 CD). CD — stale current-state statements are corrected (E-0188 CD). HI — these are substantive documented ownership/meaning corrections, not merely renaming.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — earlier placement deviations have explicit package/dependency or cycle rationales (E-0360 CD; E-0391 CD; E-0399 CD); compatibility deletion is conditioned on actual usage audits (E-0383 CD). CD — later canonical-path narrowing discloses unfinished links rather than silently asserting completion (E-0486 CD). HI — a later preferred owner does not make every earlier staged arrangement a mistake.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: search each important correction for an earlier legitimate dependency, temporary compatibility role or unchanged semantic contract. Reused module-placement/compatibility searches, E-0410's consumer-alignment account and T017/T019 distinctions; contrasted duplicate/current-state repairs with E-0360/E-0391/E-0383's justified exceptions. Result: selected mismatches are explicit, while a universal earlier-ownership-defect claim fails.
- Decision/audit links: D-018/D-030/D-032/D-033/D-057/D-062; T-04/T-26/T-27/T-28/T-44; RC-11/16; X-14/X-15/X-18/X-19.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — documentary descriptions of source placement and duplication are not independently verified code facts. Whether every move changed semantics, or whether the earlier placement was optimal under its constraints, remains case-specific.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — sharpens BF-03/BF-06 into particular corrections and strengthens PF-06/PF-07. It does not label the complete ownership lineage erroneous.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: selected ownership/completeness corrections may converge with H1/H9, with their successful correction preserved as counter-evidence or a positive finding.
- Material absence claim: No for the affirmative cases. No claim that the earlier choices lacked rationale is made.

#### H13 — Rule preservation outperformed rationale preservation

- Normalized hypothesis: Consolidations and retirements often preserved current rules or successor pointers while losing the rationale, alternatives, rejected options, historical status or explanation that made those rules understandable.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — Final-result compression deliberately drops option-by-option archaeology (E-0302 CD); coordinator compression removes explanatory examples (E-0189 CD), and a later historical onboarding state retains a rationale pointer to the handoff deleted in the same commit (E-0533 CD). CD — split-plan/review retirement intentionally sends detailed history out of live guidance (E-0437 CD; E-0509 CD). HI — T032 establishes selected rule-versus-explanation asymmetry, not pervasive loss of rationale necessary for current use.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — Failed supplement alternatives remain explained (E-0314 CD); dated states are qualified rather than erased (E-0405 CD); the rejected iterative migration strategy has an explicit retained reason (E-0447 CD). CD — the actual successor handoff preserves UUID alias ambiguity, wrapper-removal rationale, a rejected generic codec alternative and explicit value-source policy (E-0532 CD). HI — selected rationale/alternative preservation directly weakens the broad transfer-loss claim.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: seek actual explanations in destinations, not just successor pointers. Reused the cbb576b retained-handoff reopen, c8481ca1 dated-state clarification, and rationale/alternative/retired-owner search; compared these with the explicit compression policy and X-seeds. Result: material retained explanations refute a pointer-only account of those cases. Neither universal losslessness nor the pack's 'often' asymmetry follows; T032 must adjudicate each seeded transfer. Supplemental T032 comparison inspected actual successor additions for the handoff, template, scope ledger and ADR, and the 8666a15a onboarding pointer against the deleted handoff's Nodes/projection section. Result: X-07/X-16 provide bounded explanatory reduction/mismatch alongside the successful transfers; the generality/current-necessity claim remains unproved.

Their selected preservation, historical compression and source-only verification limits do not establish a rate of harmful rationale loss. T035 must assess which explanations a current reader needs; Git availability is not equivalent to live guidance.

- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: a bounded mixed transfer-quality candidate can enter §M, with explanatory-pointer/rationale reductions and strong preserved-rationale counter-evidence. No broad claim that necessary current rationale was generally lost.
- Material absence claim: Conditional: a finding about an omitted live explanation needs a bounded A-record at T033. Intentional historical removal itself is affirmative evidence and needs no invented absence claim.

#### H14 — Requirement-strength drift was sometimes implicit

- Normalized hypothesis: Some requirements moved between proposal/optional/default/required/forbidden or temporary/current/permanent states without a sufficiently explicit documentary decision explaining the strength transition.
- Final challenge classification: **INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE**
- Strongest support: CD — A required disabled-by-default inclusion boundary becomes direct default route exposure, with the old boundary later characterized as a helper (E-0142 CD; E-0153 CD; E-0154 CD). CD — the unapproved operator-endpoint draft is followed by implemented opt-in status and a changed acceptance list (E-0236 CD; E-0240 CD). HI — strength/role transitions are real; whether their rationale was insufficiently explicit is a narrower question than whether a transition occurred.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — The exposure transition states its new backend/scope and remaining hardening obligations (E-0153 CD); the opt-in endpoint receives an explicit Decision 13 with consequences and non-inferences (E-0240 CD). CD — shadow readiness is expressly superseded for lack of useful real-traffic context, local startup scope is qualified, startup modes are named, and the terminal proposal remains unapproved (E-0263 CD; E-0339 CD; E-0502 CD; E-0521 CD). HI — these are substantial explicit transitions, not silent modal drift.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: find an explicit new decision, scope qualification or rationale at the purportedly implicit transitions. Reused T017's strength audit and the lifecycle/default search; bounded documentary diffs at 3129147c (route owners) and a81ec639 (status design/observed decisions) were additionally inspected. Result: new state and constraints are explicit, including Decision 13; the inspected passages do not by themselves settle adequacy of the earlier approval/activation rationale. No claim of corpus-wide missing rationale is made.
- Decision/audit links: D-013/D-020/D-022/D-027/D-050/D-066; T-13/T-18/T-20/T-24/T-44; RC-03/07/14/19; X-05/X-09/X-20/X-24.
- Residual uncertainty: RJ — an unapproved draft is not a rule that implementation can never replace; an explicit status update is not automatically a complete rationale. The ambiguous authorization/activation cases require a material bounded absence audit if elevated into a final defect.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — retains BF-01/BF-08 as scoped questions, not established undocumented drift; explicit reversals and qualifications strengthen PF-06/PF-10. This challenge weakens a blanket implicit-strength reading.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: No negative implicit-drift finding yet. A narrower terminology/decision-continuity candidate can enter §M only if its evidentiary requirement is met; explicit scope-change positives remain eligible.
- Material absence claim: Yes, only if a proposed final finding asserts that an explanation/approval record was not found for a specific material transition. T033 must search and bound that wording first.

#### H15 — Important chronology became discoverable mainly through Git archaeology

- Normalized hypothesis: After final-state compression and retirement of historical layers, some important architectural rationale/status chronology became understandable only by reconstructing Git history rather than by reading the surviving documentary architecture.
- Final challenge classification: **PARTIALLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — Final-state compression deliberately reduces alternatives/history (E-0302 CD); the detailed split-plan layer is retired (E-0437 CD); the final documentation sends Gen1 findings to Git and moves remaining-work chronology out of the active plan (E-0509 CD; E-0522 CD). HI — some detailed decision/status chronology consequently requires historical reconstruction; that historical-access observation is supported independently of a current-readability defect.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — The retained handoff preserves selected explanations and a rejected alternative (E-0532 CD); historical snapshots can be explicitly qualified in surviving owners (E-0405 CD). CD — the final README assigns current architecture, operations and remaining work to named owners, and authoring principles define narrative versus derived authority (E-0509 CD; E-0522 CD; E-0531 CD). HI — current understanding may be self-contained even when historical reconstruction is not.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: look for surviving rationale and explicit current-owner boundaries that remove a reader's need to reconstruct Git. Reused the frozen README/spec owner read, cbb576b actual rationale inspection and c8481ca1 dated snapshot; compared intentional-history policy with current remaining-work and cutover wording. Result: selected important explanations survive and obsolete milestone chronology is deliberately historical. T035 must test necessity for the present architecture rather than infer it from retirement.
- Decision/audit links: D-018/D-040/D-063/D-066; T-04/T-31/T-44; RC-11/14/20; X-14/X-15/X-16/X-21/X-22/X-23.
- Residual uncertainty: RJ — importance to a historian and necessity to a current reader are different. This classification supports deliberate Git-dependent chronology; it does not establish that present architecture cannot be understood without hidden history.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — preserves BF-04's case-level transfer question and PF-08. It prevents retired detail from being counted automatically as current-reader harm.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Potential explanatory finding about intentional historical compression; no current-readability defect before T035. Any material omission must pass T033/§M.
- Material absence claim: Conditional on an intended claim that a necessary current explanation is absent from surviving guidance. Merely documenting deliberate history retirement does not require an absence claim.

#### H16 — Simplification pressure reflects real complexity, but the final proposal is not proof

- Normalized hypothesis: The terminal simplification proposal and preceding consolidation history may indicate that architectural/documentary/proof complexity had accumulated enough to merit reduction. However, the simplification document is explicitly UNAPPROVED and Q2-gated. Therefore it is evidence of a proposed diagnosis/alternative, NOT documentary proof that the prior architecture was objectively overengineered.
- Final challenge classification: **STRONGLY SUPPORTED**
- Strongest support: CD — Specific semantic duplication and repeated live status/module material receive targeted consolidation (E-0400 CD; E-0448 CD; E-0509 CD). CD — the terminal alternative is explicitly UNAPPROVED and gated on Q2-C (E-0519 CD; E-0521 CD). RD — its diagnosis questions packaging, proof and documentary burden (E-0520 RD). HI — evidence supports real bounded simplification targets and a proposed broader diagnosis; RJ — it does not establish objective overall overengineering.
- Strongest counter-evidence: CD — The authoring principles bound neutral abstraction with real requirements (E-0531 CD); safe cleanup preserves semantic boundaries (E-0480 CD); the proposal protects live proof, same-source comparison, explicit KEEP/DELETE/BLOCKED decisions and prerequisites (E-0521 CD). RD — it acknowledges valid fixtures and the firewall's real negative property (E-0520 RD). HI — some complexity has an explicit purpose and some consolidation already succeeded.
- Active disconfirming search: Active falsifier: test whether the proposal actually authorizes simplification or treats volume as sufficient proof, and seek necessary constraints plus completed reductions. Reused proposal UNAPPROVED/Q2/live-proof/firewall searches and frozen owner consolidation evidence; compared E-0519/E-0521 with E-0400/E-0448/E-0531. Result: the hypothesis's qualification survives strongly; its broader cost diagnosis remains a proposal, not a measured verdict.
- Decision/audit links: D-033/D-038/D-062/D-064/D-066/D-069; T-02/T-26/T-28/T-42/T-44; RC-17/18/19; X-19/X-22/X-23/X-24.
- Residual uncertainty: HI — net cost, the best alternative and the feasibility/value of proposed reductions remain unproved. A protected gate's existence is not evidence that its removal would be safe, and no implementation comparison was made.
- Effect on blind candidates: RJ — retains BF-07 as differentiated documentary/proof complexity and strengthens PF-10. It blocks promoting the proposal's retrospective critique to contemporaneous fact or treating its status as approved architecture.
- Proposed-final-finding seed: Yes: supported conditional-proposal discipline and bounded successful consolidation can seed §M. No objective-overengineering conclusion follows.
- Material absence claim: No; the qualification and concrete consolidation evidence are affirmative.

## M. Counter-Evidence

Evidence references resolve; no candidate or E-ID addition occurred. This is the authoritative proposed-final-finding ledger. Their Low rating denotes limited residual documentary qualification, not low value of the positive contribution or a claim of an implementation defect. Removed records remain here and are not reportable. Weakened/downgraded records are reportable solely at their final wording and ratings.
